{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nThis is the context manager for the LLM agent.\\nauthor: Kangrui Wang, Zihan Wang\\ndate: 2025-03-30\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom itertools import zip_longest\\nimport logging\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Union, Tuple\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\nimport re\\nfrom verl import DataProto\\nfrom verl.utils.dataset.rl_dataset import collate_fn\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\\nimport hydra\\nfrom ragen.utils import register_resolvers\\nfrom ragen.env import REGISTERED_ENV_CONFIGS\\nfrom tensordict import TensorDict\\n\\nfrom dataclasses import asdict\\nfrom ragen.llm_agent.memory.factory import create_memory\\nfrom ragen.llm_agent.memory.base import BaseMemory\\nregister_resolvers()\\n\\ndef get_special_tokens(tokenizer: AutoTokenizer):\\n    if \\\"qwen\\\" in tokenizer.name_or_path.lower():\\n        special_token = tokenizer.encode(\\\"<|im_start|>\\\")[0]\\n        reward_token = tokenizer.encode(\\\"<|im_end|>\\\")[0]\\n    elif \\\"llama-3\\\" in tokenizer.name_or_path.lower():\\n        special_token = 128006\\n        reward_token = 128009\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unsupported model: {tokenizer.name_or_path}\\\")\\n    return special_token, reward_token\\n\\ndef get_masks_and_scores(input_ids: torch.Tensor, tokenizer: AutoTokenizer, all_scores: List[List[float]] = None, use_turn_scores: bool = False, enable_response_mask: bool = False):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    input_ids: shape (bsz, seq_len)\\n    Get loss mask that only learns between <|im_start|>assistant and <|im_end|>. Currently only supports qwen.\\n    NOTE: important! This assumes that the input_ids starts with system and then user & assistant in alternative ways\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    special_token, reward_token = get_special_tokens(tokenizer)\\n    \\n    turn_starts = torch.where(input_ids == special_token, 1, 0)\\n    turn_indicators = torch.cumsum(turn_starts, dim=-1)\\n    if enable_response_mask:\\n        loss_mask = (turn_indicators % 2 == 1) & (turn_indicators > 1) # only learns all assistant turns\\n    else:\\n        loss_mask = (turn_indicators > 1) # learns everything after system prompt\\n    response_mask = (turn_indicators % 2 == 1) & (turn_indicators > 1)\\n    \\n    score_tensor = torch.zeros_like(input_ids, dtype=torch.float32)\\n    if use_turn_scores:\\n        for idx, scores in enumerate(zip_longest(*all_scores, fillvalue=0)):\\n            scores = torch.tensor(scores, dtype=torch.float32)\\n            turn_indicator = idx * 2 + 3 # 0: pad. 1: system. 2+2n: user. 3+2n: assistant\\n            reward_position = (input_ids == reward_token) & (turn_indicators == turn_indicator)\\n            # Set the last token of the rows where all positions are False to True\\n            reward_position[~reward_position.any(dim=-1), -1] = True\\n            score_tensor[reward_position] = scores\\n        if \\\"qwen\\\" in tokenizer.name_or_path.lower():\\n            # for Qwen, there is a \\\"\\\\n\\\" between special token and reward token, so we shift this to make sure reward is assigned to the last token of a turn\\n            score_tensor = score_tensor.roll(shifts=1, dims=-1)\\n    else:\\n        scores = [sum(i) for i in all_scores]\\n        score_tensor[:, -1] = torch.tensor(scores, dtype=torch.float32)\\n    score_tensor = score_tensor[:, 1:] # remove the first token\\n    loss_mask = loss_mask[:, :-1].float() # remove the last token\\n    response_mask = response_mask[:, :-1].float() # remove the last token\\n\\n    return score_tensor, loss_mask, response_mask\\n\\n\\n\\nclass ContextManager:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Manages the context for LLM interactions with environments.\\n    Translates between environment outputs and LLM inputs, and vice versa.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, \\n                 config,\\n                 tokenizer,\\n                 processor = None,\\n                 mode: str = \\\"train\\\",\\n                 ):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Initialize the ContextManager.\\n        Processor is used to process the image data.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.config = config\\n        self.tokenizer = tokenizer\\n        self.processor = processor\\n        self.mode = mode\\n        self.acti\\n\\n... Currently only supports qwen.\\n    NOTE: important! This assumes that the input_ids starts with system and then user & assistant in alternative ways\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    special_token, reward_token = get_special_tokens(tokenizer)\\n    \\n    turn_starts = torch.where(input_ids == special_token, 1, 0)\\n    turn_indicators = torch.cumsum(turn_starts, dim=-1)\\n    if enable_response_mask:\\n        loss_mask = (turn_indicators % 2 == 1) & (turn_indicators > 1) # if self.config.agent_proxy.enable_think:\\n            prompt_text += \\\"&lt;think>\\\"\\n        prompt_ids = self.tokenizer(prompt_text, add_special_tokens=False)[\\\"input_ids\\\"]\\n\\n        reasoning_text = \\\"\\\"\\n        if self.config.agent_proxy.enable_think and history:\\n            first_turn = history[0]\\n            if \\\"llm_response\\\" in first_turn:\\n                match = re.search(r\\\"&lt;think>(.*?)&lt;/think>\\\", first_turn[\\\"llm_response\\\"], re.DOTALL)\\n                if match:\\n                    reasoning_text = match.group(1)\\n        for special_token in self.special_token_list:\\n            reasoning_text = reasoning_text.replace(special_token, \\\"\\\")\\n        reasoning_text = reasoning_text.strip()\\n        reasoning_ids = self.tokenizer(reasoning_text, add_special_tokens=False)[\\\"input_ids\\\"]\\n        return prompt_ids, reasoning_ids\\n\\n    def _build_turn_prompt_and_reasoning_ids(\\n        self,\\n {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nThis is the context manager for the LLM agent.\\nauthor: Kangrui Wang, Zihan Wang\\ndate: 2025-03-30\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nfrom itertools import zip_longest\\nimport logging\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Union, Tuple\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\nimport re\\nfrom verl import DataProto\\nfrom verl.utils.dataset.rl_dataset import collate_fn\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer\\nimport hydra\\nfrom ragen.utils import register_resolvers\\nfrom ragen.env import REGISTERED_ENV_CONFIGS\\nfrom tensordict import TensorDict\\n\\nfrom dataclasses import asdict\\nfrom ragen.llm_agent.memory.factory import create_memory\\nfrom ragen.llm_agent.memory.base import BaseMemory\\nregister_resolvers()\\n\\ndef get_special_tokens(tokenizer: AutoTokenizer):\\n    if \\\"qwen\\\" in tokenizer.name_or_path.lower():\\n        special_token = tokenizer.encode(\\\"<|im_start|>\\\")[0]\\n        reward_token = tokenizer.encode(\\\"<|im_end|>\\\")[0]\\n    elif \\\"llama-3\\\" in tokenizer.name_or_path.lower():\\n        special_token = 128006\\n        reward_token = 128009\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unsupported model: {tokenizer.name_or_path}\\\")\\n    return special_token, reward_token\\n\\ndef get_masks_and_scores(input_ids: torch.Tensor, tokenizer: AutoTokenizer, all_scores: List[List[float]] = None, use_turn_scores: bool = False, enable_response_mask: bool = False):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    input_ids: shape (bsz, seq_len)\\n    Get loss mask that only learns between <|im_start|>assistant and <|im_end|>. Currently only supports qwen.\\n    NOTE: important! This assumes that the input_ids starts with system and then user & assistant in alternative ways\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    special_token, reward_token = get_special_tokens(tokenizer)\\n    \\n    turn_starts = torch.where(input_ids == special_token, 1, 0)\\n    turn_indicators = torch.cumsum(turn_starts, dim=-1)\\n    if enable_response_mask:\\n        loss_mask = (turn_indicators % 2 == 1) & (turn_indicators > 1) # only learns all assistant turns\\n    else:\\n        loss_mask = (turn_indicators > 1) # learns everything after system prompt\\n    response_mask = (turn_indicators % 2 == 1) & (turn_indicators > 1)\\n    \\n    score_tensor = torch.zeros_like(input_ids, dtype=torch.float32)\\n    if use_turn_scores:\\n        for idx, scores in enumerate(zip_longest(*all_scores, fillvalue=0)):\\n            scores = torch.tensor(scores, dtype=torch.float32)\\n            turn_indicator = idx * 2 + 3 # <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
Now I have the key pieces:

**RAGEN ctx_manager.py format:**
1. **System prompt**: `"You're a helpful assistant. " + env_instruction` where for AlfworldSG, env_instruction is the multi-line text about ALFRED environment, self-guidance assessment, think tags, etc.
2. **Format prompt**: `"&lt;think> [Your thoughts] &lt;/think> &lt;answer> [your answer] &lt;/answer>"` (when `enable_think` is True)
3. **User message**: Uses `_build_turn_state_content` which formats as:
   ```
   Turn N:
   State:
   {turn['state']}
   {warning}You have {actions_left} actions left. Always output: &lt;think> [Your thoughts] &lt;/think> &lt;answer> [your answer] &lt;/answer> with no extra text. Strictly follow this format. Max response length: {max_tokens} words (tokens).
   ```

**eval_checkpoint.py current format:**
1. **System prompt**: Hardcoded `SYSTEM_PROMPT` that's similar to the env_instruction but WITHOUT the `"You're a helpful assistant. "` prefix
2. **User message**: Just raw `obs` text, no `Turn N:` / `State:` / actions_left formatting

So the main difference is that RAGEN wraps everything in structured turn-based prompts with explicit formatting instructions and action counts, while eval_checkpoint.py uses a simpler approach with just the raw observation text and a different system prompt structure.

There's also a contradiction I'm noticing: the eval script's system prompt tells the model "Do NOT wrap the action in any tags," but the action parsing actually expects `&lt;answer>` tags. This mismatch between what the prompt says and what the parser accepts could be causing issues.

Let me trace through the exp027 config to understand how it's building messages. It uses `step_independent` context window mode, which likely affects how the conversation history is formatted. I should check the context manager to see exactly how that mode constructs the message sequence.

I'm also curious about the SFT checkpoint being used — `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/qwen3_exp021_ragen_sft_v4` — since that's a fine-tuned model. I need to look at what training data was used to understand what format it expects.

Looking at the actual issue, it seems there are three concrete fixes needed: the system prompt needs the "You're a helpful assistant." prefix that was in the training data, the user message format should be structured with Turn labels and State sections instead of raw observations, and the action format should consistently use `&lt;think>` and `&lt;answer>` tags throughout. I see there's a contradiction between the env_instruction telling the model not to wrap actions in tags, and the format prompt that requires `&lt;answer>` tags when `enable_think` is enabled. The format prompt gets injected into the user message itself, so during training the model would see both directives. Let me check if exp027 actually has `enable_think` turned on by looking at its config and the base configuration it references. I'm noticing a contradiction in the training setup—the system prompt tells the model not to use tags, but the user message format explicitly requires `&lt;answer>` tags, so the model would learn from whichever format actually appears in the training data. Looking at how the eval script structures the conversation, it builds a message history by appending assistant responses and new observations in sequence, though I need to see the complete implementation to understand how it parses and executes actions across multiple steps.

\\\"pick_two_obj_and_place\\\": \\\"put\\\",\\n    \\\"pick_heat_then_place_in_recep\\\": \\\"heat\\\",\\n    \\\"pick_cool_then_place_in_recep\\\": \\\"cool\\\",\\n    \\\"pick_clean_then_place_in_recep\\\": \\\"clean\\\",\\n    \\\"look_at_obj_in_light\\\": \\\"examine\\\",\\n}\\n\\nMAX_PROMPT_TOKENS = 30000\\n\\n\\ndef parse_action(response: str):\\n    has_think = bool(re.search(r\\\"&lt;think>.*?&lt;/think>\\\", response, re.DOTALL))\\n    m = re.search(r\\\"&lt;think>.*?&lt;/think>\\\\s*&lt;answer>(.*?)&lt;/answer>\\\", response, re.DOTALL)\\n    if m:\\n        return m.group(1).strip(), has_think, True\\n    m = re.search(r\\\"&lt;/think>\\\\s*(.*)\\\", response, re.DOTALL)\\n    if m:\\n        action = m.group(1).strip().split(\\\"\\\\n\\\")[0].strip()\\n        for tok in [\\\"<|im_end|>\\\", \\\"<|endoftext|>\\\"]:\\n            action = action.replace(tok, \\\"\\\").strip()\\n        if action:\\n            return action, has_think, False\\n    return \\\"\\\", has_think, False\\n\\n\\ndef get_task_type(game_file):\\n    if not game_file:\\n        return \\\"unknown\\\"\\n    for prefix, ttype in TASK_TYPE_MAP.items():\\n        if prefix in game_file:\\n            return ttype\\n    return \\\"unknown\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef truncate_messages(messages, tokenizer, max_tokens):\\n    text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(messages, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True)\\n    token_count = len(tokenizer.encode(text))\\n    if token_count <= max_tokens:\\n        return messages\\n    preserved = messages[:2]\\n    rest = messages[2:]\\n    while rest and token_count > max_tokens:\\n        removed = min(2, len(rest))\\n        rest = rest[removed:]\\n        trial = preserved + rest\\n        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(trial, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True)\\n        token_count = len(tokenizer.encode(text))\\n    return preserved + env.reset(seed=episode_idx, mode=\\\"val\\\")\\n    game_file = getattr(env, \\\"current_game_file\\\", \\\"\\\")\\n    task_type = get_task_type(game_file)\\n\\n    messages = [\\n  msgs = truncate_messages(messages, tokenizer, MAX_PROMPT_TOKENS)\\n        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True)\\n        outputs = llm.generate([text], sampling_params, use_tqdm=False)\\n        response = outputs[0].outputs[0].text\\n\\n        clean_response = response.replace(\\\"<|im_end|>\\\",     action_is_valid = action in env.available_actions if action else False\\n\\n        step_info = {\\n            \\\"step\\\": step_i,\\n            \\\"action\\\": action,\\n            \\\"has_think\\\": has_think,\\n            \\\"has_answer\\\": has_answer,\\n            \\\"format_ok\\\": format_ok,\\n            \\\"action_is_valid\\\": action_is_valid,\\n            \\\"action_is_effective\\\": False,\\n        }\\n\\n        if not action:\\n            step_info[\\\"success\\\"] = False\\n            step_info[\\\"done\\\"] = True\\n False)\\n        steps.append(step_info)\\n\\n        if done:\\n            break\\n\\n        messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"assistant\\\", \\\"content\\\": clean_response})\\n        messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": obs})\\n\\n    return steps, task_type\\n\\n\\ndef detect_checkpoint_format(path):\\n    if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(path, \\\"action_is_effective\\\": False,\\n        }\\n\\n        if not action:\\n            step_info[\\\"success\\\"] = False\\n            step_info[\\\"done\\\"] = True\\n            step_info[\\\"reward\\\"] = 0.0\\n            steps.append(step_info)\\n            break\\n\\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\\n        step_info[\\\"reward\\\"] = reward\\n False)\\n        steps.append(step_info)\\n\\n        if done:\\n            break\\n\\n        messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"assistant\\\", \\\"content\\\": clean_response})\\n        messages.append({\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": obs})\\n\\n    return steps, task_type\\n\\n\\ndef detect_checkpoint_format(path):\\n    if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(path, Mode mapping: RAGEN mode -> AlfWorld train_eval\\n    MODE_MAP = {\\n        'train': 'train',\\n        'val': None,  # Will use config.eval_dataset\\n        'test': None,  # Will use config.eval_dataset\\n    }\\n\\n    def __init__(self, config: AlfredEnvConfig = AlfredEnvConfig(), mode='train'):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Initialize AlfWorld environment.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            config: AlfredEnvConfig instance\\n            mode: 'train' or 'val' - determines which dataset split to use\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.config = config\\n        self.raw_env_config = load_config(self.config.config_file)\\n\\n        # Map RAGEN mode to AlfWorld train_eval\\n        self.current_mode = mode\\n        self._alfworld_mode = self._get_alfworld_mode(mode)\\n\\n        # Initialize raw environment (thread-safe cache)\\n        self.raw_env = self._get_cached_raw_env(self._alfworld_mode)\\n        self.num_games = self.raw_env.num_games\\n        self.game_files = list(self.raw_env.game_files)\\n\\n        self.alfred_env = None\\n        self.current_game_file = None\\n        self.render_cache = None\\n        self.available_actions = None\\n        self.valid_action_count = 0\\n        self.render_mode = self.config.render_mode\\n        assert self.render_mode == 'text'\\n\\n    def _get_alfworld_mode(self, mode: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert RAGEN mode to AlfWorld train_eval string.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if mode == 'train':\\n            return 'train'\\n        else:\\n            # Use eval_dataset from config for val/test modes\\n            return self.config.eval_dataset\\n\\n    def _get_cached_raw_env(self, alfworld_mode: str) -> AlfredTWEnv:\\n        cache_key = (os.path.abspath(self.config.config_file), alfworld_mode)\\n        with _RAW_ENV_LOCK:\\n            raw_env = _RAW_ENV_CACHE.get(cache_key)\\n            if raw_env is None:\\n                raw_env = AlfredTWEnv(config=self.raw_env_config, train_eval=alfworld_mode)\\n                _RAW_ENV_CACHE[cache_key] = raw_env\\n        return raw_env\\n    \\n    def _reinitialize_for_mode(self, mode: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Reinitialize the environment when mode changes.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        new_alfworld_mode = self._get_alfworld_mode(mode)\\n\\n        if new_alfworld_mode != self._alfworld_mode:\\n            # Close existing environment\\n            if hasattr(self, 'alfred_env') and self.alfred_env is not None:\\n                try:\\n                    self.alfred_env.close()\\n                except Exception:\\n                    pass\\n\\n            # Reinitialize with new mode\\n            self._alfworld_mode = new_alfworld_mode\\n            self.current_mode = mode\\n            self.raw_env = self._get_cached_raw_env(self._alfworld_mode)\\n            self.num_games = self.raw_env.num_games\\n            self.game_files = list(self.raw_env.game_files)\\n            self.alfred_env = None\\n\\n    def reset(self, seed=None, mode=None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Reset the environment with a specific seed.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            seed: Random seed for game selection\\n            mode: 'train', 'val', or 'test' - if different from current mode,\\n                  reinitializes the environment with the \\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 1336 字符] ...\\n\\nld.EnvInfos(won=True, admissible_commands=True, extras=[\\\"gamefile\\\"])\\n                wrappers = [AlfredDemangler(), AlfredInfos()]\\n                max_steps = self.raw_env_config[\\\"rl\\\"][\\\"training\\\"][\\\"max_nb_steps_per_episode\\\"]\\n\\n                env_id = textworld.gym.register_game(\\n                    selected_game,\\n                    request_infos=request_infos,\\n                    batch_size=1,\\n                    asynchronous=False,\\n                    max_episode_steps=max_steps,\\n                    wrappers=wrappers\\n                )\\n\\n                self.alfred_env = textworld.gym.make(env_id)\\n\\n                obs, info = self.alfred_env.reset()\\n            self.available_actions = info[\\\"admissible_commands\\\"][0]\\n            self.instruction_text = obs[0]\\n            self.valid_action_count = 0\\n            # Include admissible actions in render cache\\n            self.render_cache = self._format_observation(obs[0], self.available_actions)\\n            return self.render_cache\\n\\n        except (RuntimeError, RuntimeWarning) as e:\\n            print(f\\\"Error in reset: {e}\\\")\\n            next_seed = abs(hash(str(seed))) % (2 ** 32) if seed is not None else None\\n            return self.reset(next_seed, mode=self.current_mode)\\n\\n    def _format_observation(self, obs: str, admissible_actions: list) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Format observation to include admissible actions.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        actions_str = \\\", \\\".join(admissible_actions)\\n        return f\\\"{obs}\\\\n\\\\nAdmissible actions: [{actions_str}]\\\"\\n    \\n    def step(self, action: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Take a step in the environment using the provided action string.\\n\\n        Reward scheme (matching verl-agent):\\n        - reward = config.score * won (10.0 on success, 0.0 otherwise)\\n\\n        Args:\\n            action: The action string to execute\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            observation: Updated observation with admissible actions\\n            reward: Sparse reward (score on success, 0 otherwise)\\n            done: Whether episode is finished\\n            info: Additional information dict\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        action_is_available = action in self.available_actions\\n\\n        # Execute action in environment\\n        obs, _, dones, infos = self.alfred_env.step([action])\\n        observation = obs[0]\\n        self.available_actions = infos[\\\"admissible_commands\\\"][0]\\n        done = dones[0]\\n        won = infos[\\\"won\\\"][0]\\n\\n        # Track valid actions for metrics\\n        if action_is_available:\\n            self.valid_action_count += 1\\n\\n        # Reward computation\\n        reward = self.config.score * float(won)\\n        if self.config.valid_action_bonus > 0:\\n            if self.config.success_gated_bonus:\\n                # Success-gated: bonus only awarded on successful episode completion\\n                if done and won:\\n                    reward += self.valid_action_count * self.config.valid_action_bonus\\n            else:\\n                # Unconditional: bonus per valid action step\\n                if action_is_available:\\n                    reward += self.config.valid_action_bonus\\n\\n        # Format observation with admissible actions\\n        self.render_cache = self._format_observation(observation, self.available_actions)\\n\\n        info = {\\n            \\\"action_is_effective\\\": True,\\n            \\\"action_is_valid\\\": action_is_available,\\n            \\\"success\\\": won,\\n            \\\"anchor_obs\\\": observation,\\n        }\\n\\n        return self.render_cache, reward, done, info\\n    \\n    def render(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return current observation with admissible actions.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return self.render_cache\\n\\n    def close(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Clean up environment resources.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.render_cache = None\\n        if hasattr(self, 'alfred_env') and self.alfred_env is not None:\\n            try:\\n                self.alfred_env.close()\\n            except Exception:\\n                pass\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    import os\\n    os.environ[\\\"ALFWORLD_DATA\\\"] = os.path.expanduser(\\\"~/.cache/alfworld\\\")\\n\\n    # Test basic environment functionality\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"}

response_length\\n\\n        while True:\\n            messages = self._build_single_turn_messages(\\n                env_output=env_output,\\n                history=history,\\n                turn_idx=turn_idx,\\n                history_start=history_start,\\n                turn_offset=turn_offset,\\n                include_warning=include_warning,\\n                include_assistant=include_assistant,\\n            )\\n            text = self.tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\\n                messages,\\n                add_generation_prompt=add_generation_prompt,\\n                tokenize=False,\\n            )\\n            if add_generation_prompt and not include_assistant:\\n                if self.biavr_enabled:\\n                    pass  # B-IAVR: no forced prefix\\n                elif self.config.agent_proxy.enable_think:\\n                    text += \\\"&lt;think>\\\"\\n                else:\\n                    text += \\\"&lt;answer>\\\"\\n            token_len = self._count_tokens(text)\\n            if token_len <= max_length or history_start >= turn_idx:\\n                return history_start\\n            history_start += 1\\n\\n    def _build_turn_state_content(\\n        self,\\n        turn: Dict,\\n        turn_number: int,\\n        env_id: int,\\n        include_warning: bool = False,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Build state content for a single turn.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        FORMAT_PROMPT, LENGTH_PROMPT = self._build_format_prompt(env_id)\\n        warning = \\\"\\\"\\n        if include_warning and turn.get('manager_invalid_action'):\\n            warning = \\\"No valid action provided previously. {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"import re\\nfrom typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional\\nfrom .base import BaseMemory\\n\\n\\nclass AlfWorldMemory(BaseMemory):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ALFWorld-specific memory implementation using verl-agent style formatting.\\n\\n    Features:\\n    - Extracts task description from first observation\\n    - Cleans observations (removes welcome message, format instructions, etc.)\\n    - Uses compact history format: [Observation N: '...', Action N: '...']\\n    - Only shows admissible actions for current turn\\n\\n    Format:\\n    Your task is to: &lt;task>.\\n    Prior to this step, you have already taken N step(s).\\n    Below are the most recent K observations and the corresponding actions you took:\\n    [Observation 1: '...', Action 1: '...']\\n    ...\\n    You are now at step N+1 and your current observation is: &lt;obs>\\n    Your admissible actions of the current situation are: [...]\\n    Now it's your turn to take an action...\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    # Patterns to remove from observations\\n    CLEAN_PATTERNS = [\\n        r\\\"-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-\\\",\\n        r\\\"You have \\\\d+ actions left\\\\.?\\\",\\n        r\\\"Always output:.*?(?=\\\\n|$)\\\",\\n        r\\\"Strictly follow this format\\\\..*?(?=\\\\n|$)\\\",\\n        r\\\"Max response length:.*?(?=\\\\n|$)\\\",\\n    ]\\n\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        self._data = None\\n        self.batch_size = 0\\n        self._tasks = None  # Extracted task descriptions per environment\\n\\n    def reset(self, batch_size: int):\\n        if self._data is not None:\\n            self._data.clear()\\n        self._data = [[] for _ in range(batch_size)]\\n        self._tasks = [None] * batch_size\\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\\n\\n    def store(self, record: Dict[str, List[Any]]):\\n        for env_idx in range(self.batch_size):\\n            self._data[env_idx].append({k: record[k][env_idx] for k in record.keys()})\\n\\n    def _clean_observation(self, obs: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Remove boilerplate from observation text.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        cleaned = obs\\n        for pattern in self.CLEAN_PATTERNS:\\n            cleaned = re.sub(pattern, \\\"\\\", cleaned, flags=re.MULTILINE | re.DOTALL)\\n\\n        # Remove admissible actions section\\n        cleaned = re.sub(r\\\"Admissible actions:.*?(?=\\\\n|$)\\\", \\\"\\\", cleaned, flags=re.DOTALL)\\n\\n        # Clean up excessive whitespace\\n        cleaned = re.sub(r\\\"\\\\n\\\\s*\\\\n+\\\", \\\"\\\\n\\\", cleaned).strip()\\n        return cleaned\\n\\n    def _extract_task(self, state: str) -> Optional[str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract task description from state.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        match = re.search(r\\\"Your task is to: (.+?)(?:\\\\.\\\\n|\\\\n|$)\\\", state, re.DOTALL)\\n        return match.group(1).strip() if match else None\\n\\n    def _extract_admissible_actions(self, state: str) -> Optional[str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract admissible actions list from state.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        match = re.search(r\\\"Admissible actions: \\\\[(.+?)\\\\]\\\", state, re.DOTALL)\\n        return match.group(1).strip() if match else None\\n\\n    def _extract_action_from_response(self, llm_response: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract clean action from LLM response (removes think/answer tags).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        match = re.search(r'&lt;answer>(.*?)&lt;/answer>', llm_response, re.DOTALL)\\n        return match.group(1).strip() if match else llm_response.strip()\\n\\n    def build_user_content(\\n        self,\\n        env_output: Dict,\\n        history: List[Dict],\\n        turn_idx: int,\\n        history_start: int,\\n        turn_offset: int,\\n        include_warning: bool,\\n        format_prompt: str,\\n        length_prompt: str,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Build user content using verl-agent style compact format.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        env_id = env_output.get(\\\"env_id\\\", 0)\\n        content_parts = []\\n\\n        # 1. Extract and include task description (from first state)\\n        if history:\\n            if self._tasks[env_id % self.batch_size] is None:\\n                task = self._extract_task(history[0].get('state', ''))\\n                self._tasks[env_id % self.batch_size] = task\\n            task = self._tasks[env_id % self.batch_size]\\n            if task:\\n                content_parts.append(f\\\"Your task is to: {task}.\\\")\\n\\n        # 2. Step count summary\\n        total_steps = turn_idx + turn_offset\\n        if total_steps > 0:\\n            content_parts.append(\\n                f\\\"Prior to this step, you have already taken {total_steps} step(s).\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        # 3. Compact history section\\n        if history_start < turn_idx:\\n            history_count = turn_idx - history_start\\n            content_parts.append(\\n                f\\\"Below are the most recent {history_count} observations and \\\"\\n                \\\"the corresponding actions you took:\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n            for h_idx in range(history_start, turn_idx):\\n                h_turn = history[h_idx]\\n                actual_turn = h_idx + 1 + turn_offset\\n\\n                # Clean observation\\n                clean_obs = self._clean_observation(h_turn.get('state', ''))\\n                # Truncate if too long\\n                if len(clean_obs) > 200:\\n                    clean_obs = clean_obs[:197] + \\\"...\\\"\\n\\n                # Extract action from response\\n                action = self._extract_action_from_response(h_turn.get('llm_response', ''))\\n\\n                content_parts.append(\\n                    f\\\"[Observation {actual_turn}: '{clean_obs}', Action {actual_turn}: '{action}']\\\"\\n                )\\n\\n        # 4. Current step and observation\\n        current_turn = turn_idx + 1 + turn_offset\\n        current_state = history[turn_idx].get('state', '')\\n        clean_current = self._clean_observation(current_state)\\n        content_parts.append(\\n            f\\\"You are now at step {current_turn} and your current observation is: {clean_current}\\\"\\n        )\\n\\n        # 5. Current admissible actions\\n        admissible = self._extract_admissible_actions(current_state)\\n        if admissible:\\n            content_parts.append(\\n                f\\\"Your admissible actions of the current situation are: [{admissible}]\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        # 6. Warning for invalid action\\n        if include_warning and history[turn_idx].get('manager_invalid_action'):\\n            content_parts.append(\\n                \\\"No valid action provided previously. Environment state remains the same.\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        # 7. Format instructions\\n        content_parts.append(\\n            f\\\"Now it's your turn to take an action. \\\"\\n            f\\\"You have {history[turn_idx]['actions_left']} actions left. \\\"\\n            f\\\"Output your reasoning in &lt;think>...&lt;/think> tags, then output exactly one admissible action command directly after &lt;/think>. \\\"\\n            f\\\"Do not use &lt;answer> tags. The action must be a precise ALFWorld command. {length_prompt}\\\"\\n        )\\n\\n        return \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(content_parts)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"}
```

Extracted task descriptions per environment\\n\\n    def reset(self, batch_size: int):\\n        if self._data is not None:\\n            self._data.clear()\\n        self._data = [[] for _ in range(batch_size)]\\n        self._tasks = [None] * batch_size\\n        self.batch_size = batch_size\\n\\n    def store(self, record: Dict[str, List[Any]]):\\n        for env_idx in range(self.batch_size):\\n            self._data[env_idx].append({k: record[k][env_idx] for k in record.keys()})\\n\\n    def _clean_observation(self, obs: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Remove boilerplate from observation text.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        cleaned = obs\\n        for pattern in self.CLEAN_PATTERNS:\\n            cleaned = re.sub(pattern, \\\"\\\", cleaned, flags=re.MULTILINE | re.DOTALL)\\n\\n        # Remove admissible actions section\\n        cleaned = re.sub(r\\\"Admissible actions:.*?(?=\\\\n|$)\\\", \\\"\\\", cleaned, flags=re.DOTALL)\\n\\n        # Clean up excessive whitespace\\n        cleaned = re.sub(r\\\"\\\\n\\\\s*\\\\n+\\\", \\\"\\\\n\\\", cleaned).strip()\\n        return cleaned\\n\\n    def _extract_task(self, state: str) -> Optional[str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract task description from state.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        match = re.search(r\\\"Your task is to: (.+?)(?:\\\\.\\\\n|\\\\n|$)\\\", state, re.DOTALL)\\n        return match.group(1).strip() if match else None\\n\\n    def _extract_admissible_actions(self, state: str) -> Optional[str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract admissible actions list from state.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        match = re.search(r\\\"Admissible actions: \\\\[(.+?)\\\\]\\\", state, re.DOTALL)\\n        return match.group(1).strip() if match else None\\n\\n    def _extract_action_from_response(self, llm_response: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract clean action from LLM response (removes think/answer tags).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        match = re.search(r'&lt;answer>(.*?)&lt;/answer>', llm_response, re.DOTALL)\\n        return match.group(1).strip() if match else llm_response.strip()\\n\\n    def build_user_content(\\n        self,\\n        env_output: Dict,\\n        history: List[Dict],\\n        turn_idx: int,\\n        history_start: int,\\n        turn_offset: int,\\n        include_warning: bool,\\n        format_prompt: str,\\n        length_prompt: str,\\n    ) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Build user content using verl-agent style compact format.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        env_id = env_output.get(\\\"env_id\\\", 0)\\n        content_parts = []\\n\\n        # 1. Extract and include task description (from first state)\\n        if history:\\n            if self._tasks[env_id % self.batch_size] is None:\\n                task = self._extract_task(history[0].get('state', ''))\\n                self._tasks[env_id % self.batch_size] = task\\n            task = self._tasks[env_id % self.batch_size]\\n            if task:\\n                content_parts.append(f\\\"Your task is to: {task}.\\\")\\n\\n        # 2. Step count summary\\n        total_steps = turn_idx + turn_offset\\n        if total_steps > 0:\\n            content_parts.append(\\n                f\\\"Prior to this step, you have already taken {total_steps} step(s).\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        # 3. Compact history section\\n        if history_start < turn_idx:\\n            history_count = turn_idx - history_start\\n            content_parts.append(\\n                f\\\"Below are the most recent {history_count} observations and \\\"\\n                \\\"the corresponding actions you took:\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n            for h_idx in range(history_start, turn_idx):\\n                h_turn = history[h_idx]\\n                actual_turn = h_idx + 1 + turn_offset\\n\\n                # Clean observation\\n                clean_obs = self._clean_observation(h_turn.get('state', ''))\\n                # Truncate if too long\\n                if len(clean_obs) > 200:\\n                    clean_obs = clean_obs[:197] + \\\"...\\\"\\n\\n                # Extract action from response\\n                action = self._extract_action_from_response(h_turn.get('llm_response', ''))\\n\\n                content_parts.append(\\n                    f\\\"[Observation {actual_turn}: '{clean_obs}', Action {actual_turn}: '{action}']\\\"\\n                )\\n\\n        # 4. Current step and observation\\n        current_turn = turn_idx + 1 + turn_offset\\n        current_state = history[turn_idx].get('state', '')\\n        clean_current = self._clean_observation(current_state)\\n        content_parts.append(\\n            f\\\"You are now at step {current_turn} and your current observation is: {clean_current}\\\"\\n        )\\n\\n        # 5. Current admissible actions\\n        admissible = self._extract_admissible_actions(current_state)\\n        if admissible:\\n            content_parts.append(\\n                f\\\"Your admissible actions of the current situation are: [{admissible}]\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        # 6. Warning for invalid action\\n        if include_warning and history[turn_idx].get('manager_invalid_action'):\\n            content_parts.append(\\n                \\\"No valid action provided previously. Environment state remains the same.\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        # 7. Format instructions\\n        content_parts.append(\\n            f\\\"Now it's your turn to take an action. \\\"\\n            f\\\"You have {history[turn_idx]['actions_left']} actions left. \\\"\\n            f\\\"Output your reasoning in &lt;think>...&lt;/think> tags, then output exactly one admissible action command directly after &lt;/think>. \\\"\\n            f\\\"Do not use &lt;answer> tags. The action must be a precise ALFWorld command. {length_prompt}\\\"\\n        )\\n\\n        return \\\"\\\\n\\\".join(content_parts)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.36}}"
    }
  }
}

Now I have a complete understanding. Let me also check the _parse_response function. I can see that in the ctx_manager, the model's response is parsed and normalized to `&lt;think>...&lt;/think>&lt;answer>...&lt;/answer>` format. This normalized response is what gets stored in `history[turn_idx]['llm_response']`.

So during GRPO training with step_independent mode, the model sees:

**System message:** `"You're a helpful assistant. " + env_instruction`

**User message (built by AlfWorldMemory.build_user_content):**
```


Your task is to: {task}.
Prior to this step, you have already taken {N} step(s).
Below are the most recent {K} observations and the corresponding actions you took:
[Observation 1: '{clean_obs}', Action 1: '{action}']
...
You are now at step {current_step} and your current observation is: {clean_current}
Your admissible actions of the current situation are: [{admissible}]
Now it's your turn to take an action. You have {actions_left} actions left. Output your reasoning in &lt;think>...&lt;/think> tags, then output exactly one admissible action command directly after &lt;/think>.
```

I'm noticing a discrepancy in the instructions being passed to the model. The AlfWorldMemory class is hardcoding its own format requirements that explicitly say not to use `&lt;answer>` tags and to output the action command directly after the closing `&lt;/think>` tag. But there's a FORMAT_PROMPT parameter being passed from `_build_single_turn_user_content` that specifies a different format with `&lt;answer>` tags, which the memory class is completely ignoring. This means the actual instructions the model receives don't match what the format prompt variable suggests should be used.

However, the response parsing function handles both formats gracefully—it can parse either the `&lt;think>...&lt;/think>&lt;answer>...&lt;/answer>` pattern or the bare text fallback, and it normalizes everything to the answer-tag format for storage in history. So even though the model is being told not to use answer tags, the system can still handle responses that do include them. The current eval script's system prompt also aligns with the AlfWorldMemory's instruction to not wrap the action in tags, so there's consistency there at least.

But wait, I'm noticing something odd in the AlfWorldMemory's build_user_content method—it explicitly tells the model to output reasoning in think tags and then place the action directly after the closing tag without answer tags. Yet the parsing function is normalizing everything to include answer tags when storing it. That seems like a mismatch between what the model is being instructed to do and how the responses are being processed.

Let me trace through what's actually happening during training. The memory manager is what produces the formatted prompts the model sees, and AlfWorldMemory is using its own format instructions that don't include answer tags. But the issue description mentions that training data does use answer tags, so there's a contradiction somewhere between what the model was trained on and what the current eval script is asking for.

Looking at the GRPO training flow, responses get normalized to include answer tags, and those tagged responses end up in the history for subsequent steps. However, when AlfWorldMemory builds its compact format for the next training iteration, it strips those tags out. So the actual training data being generated now doesn't have answer tags in the instructions, even though the base model might have been trained on data that did.

For the eval script, I need to match exactly what the model learned from during GRPO training. That means using the same system prompt structure with the helpful assistant prefix, formatting the user message in AlfWorldMemory's compact style, and keeping the format instructions consistent with what was actually in the training data.

The key misalignment is that the current eval_checkpoint.py uses raw observation text, but RAGEN's training pipeline goes through AlfWorldMemory.build_user_content, which produces a different format. I need to replicate that same message construction in the eval script so the model sees familiar input during evaluation. to be restructured for single-turn format. I need to update the system prompt to include the assistant prefix and refactor how messages are constructed during episodes, moving from multi-turn chat history to packing everything into a single user message with compact history embedded.

{
  "server": "bjb1-17606",

script's build_user_content\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/cost-verify-agent-rl/scripts\")\nimport importlib.util\nspec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(\"eval_checkpoint\", \"/root/cost-verify-agent-rl/scripts/eval_checkpoint.py\")\n# Can't import directly due to multiprocessing set_start_method, so just test the functions\nimport re\n\nCLEAN_PATTERNS = [\n    r\"-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-\",\n    r\"You have \\d+ actions left\\.?\",\n    r\"Always output:.*?(?=\\n|$)\",\n    r\"Strictly follow this format\\..*?(?=\\n|$)\",\n    r\"Max response length:.*?(?=\\n|$)\",\n]\n\ndef clean_observation(obs):\n    cleaned = obs\n    for pattern in CLEAN_PATTERNS:\n        cleaned = re.sub(pattern, \"\", cleaned, flags=re.MULTILINE | re.DOTALL)\n    cleaned = re.sub(r\"Admissible actions:.*?(?=\\n|$)\", \"\", cleaned, flags=re.DOTALL)\n    cleaned = re.sub(r\"\\n\\s*\\n+\", \"\\n\", cleaned).strip()\n    return cleaned\n\ndef extract_task(state):\n    match = re.search(r\"Your task is to: (.+?)(?:\\.\\n|\\n|$)\", state, re.DOTALL)\n    return match.group(1).strip() if match else None\n\ndef extract_admissible_actions(state):\n    match = re.search(r\"Admissible actions: \\[(.+?)\\]\", state, re.DOTALL)\n    return match.group(1).strip() if match else None\n\ndef extract_action_from_response(llm_response):\n    match = re.search(r'&lt;answer>(.*?)&lt;/answer>', llm_response, re.DOTALL)\n    if match:\n        return match.group(1).strip()\n    match = re.search(r'&lt;/think>\\s*(.*)', llm_response, re.DOTALL)\n    if match:\n        action = match.group(1).strip().split(\"\\n\")[0].strip()\n        for tok in [\"<|im_end|>\", \"<|endoftext|>\"]:\n            action = action.replace(tok, \"\").strip()\n        return action\n    return llm_response.strip()\n\nHISTORY_LENGTH = 5\nLENGTH_PROMPT = \"Max response length: 512 words (tokens).\"\n\ndef build_user_content(obs, history, step_idx, max_steps, task_desc=None):\n    content_parts = []\n    if task_desc:\n        content_parts.append(f\"Your task is to: {task_desc}.\")\n    if step_idx > 0:\n        content_parts.append(f\"Prior to this step, you have already taken {step_idx} step(s).\")\n    history_start = max(0, len(history) - HISTORY_LENGTH)\n    visible_history = history[history_start:]\n    if visible_history:\n        content_parts.append(\n            f\"Below are the most recent {len(visible_history)} observations and \"\n            \"the corresponding actions you took:\"\n        )\n        for h in visible_history:\n            h_obs = clean_observation(h[\"obs\"])\n            if len(h_obs) > 200:\n                h_obs = h_obs[:197] + \"...\"\n            h_action = extract_action_from_response(h[\"response\"])\n            content_parts.append(\n                f\"[Observation {h['step']}: '{h_obs}', Action {h['step']}: '{h_action}']\"\n            )\n    current_step = step_idx + 1\n    clean_current = clean_observation(obs)\n    content_parts.append(\n        f\"You are now at step {current_step} and your current observation is: {clean_current}\"\n    )\n    admissible = extract_admissible_actions(obs)\n    if admissible:\n        content_parts.append(\n            f\"Your admissible actions of the current situation are: [{admissible}]\"\n        )\n    actions_left = max_steps - step_idx\n    content_parts.append(\n        f\"Now it's your turn to take an action. \"\n        f\"You have {actions_left} actions left. \"\n        f\"Output your reasoning in &lt;think>...&lt;/think> tags, then output exactly one admissible action command directly after &lt;/think>. \"\n        f\"Do not use &lt;answer> tags. The action must be a precise ALFWorld command. {LENGTH_PROMPT}\"\n    )\n    return \"\\n\".join(content_parts)\n\n# Test: first turn\nobs0 = \"-= Welcome to TextWorld, ALFRED! =-\\n\\nYou are in the middle of a room. Looking quickly around you, you see a cabinet 4, a cabinet 3, a cabinet 2, a cabinet 1, a countertop 1, a garbagecan 1, a handtowelholder 2, a handtowelholder 1, a sinkbasin 2, a sinkbasin 1, a toilet 1, a toiletpaperhanger 1, and a towelholder 1.\\n\\nYour task is to: put some spraybottle on toilet.\\n\\nAdmissible actions: [go to cabinet 1, go to cabinet 2, go to cabinet 3, go to cabinet 4, go to countertop 1, go to garbagecan 1, go to handtowelholder 1, go to handtowelholder 2, go to sinkbasin 1, go to sinkbasin 2, go to toilet 1, go to toiletpaperhanger 1, go to towelholder 1]\"\n\nprint(\"=== TURN 0 (first turn) ===\")\nprint(build_user_content(obs0, [], 0, 50, extract_task(obs0)))\nprint()\n\n# Test: turn 3 with history\nhistory = [\n    {\"step\": 1, \"obs\": obs0, \"response\": \"&lt;think>[Assessment: neutral] I need to find a spraybottle.&lt;/think>go to cabinet 1\"},\n    {\"step\": 2, \"obs\": \"You arrive at cabinet 1. The cabinet 1 is closed.\\n\\nAdmissible actions: [open cabinet 1, go to cabinet 2]\",\n     \"response\": \"&lt;think>[Assessment: neutral] Cabinet is closed, let me open it.&lt;/think>open cabinet 1\"},\n    {\"step\": 3, \"obs\": \"You open the cabinet 1. The cabinet 1 is open. In it, you see a cloth 1.\\n\\nAdmissible actions: [close cabinet 1, go to cabinet 2]\",\n     \"response\": \"&lt;think>[Assessment: negative] No spraybottle here.&lt;/think>go to cabinet 2\"},\n]\nobs3 = \"You arrive at cabinet 2. The cabinet 2 is closed.\\n\\nAdmissible actions: [open cabinet 2, go to cabinet 3]\"\nprint(\"=== TURN 3 (with history) ===\")\nprint(build_user_content(obs3, history, 3, 50, extract_task(obs0)))\n\n# System prompt check\nENV_INSTRUCTION = (\n    \"You are an expert agent in the ALFRED Embodied Environment.\\n\"\n    \"Complete household tasks by navigating and interacting with objects.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Before each action, you MUST first produce a self-guidance assessment in your reasoning:\\n\"\n    \"[Assessment: positive/neutral/negative] - evaluate your current progress toward the task goal.\\n\"\n    \"[Reasoning: one sentence analyzing what has been accomplished and what remains.]\\n\"\n    \"[Suggestion: the best next action from admissible actions.]\\n\\n\"\n    \"Your reasoning MUST be enclosed within &lt;think> &lt;/think> tags.\\n\"\n    \"After the closing &lt;/think> tag, output EXACTLY ONE admissible action command directly.\\n\"\n    \"The action must be a precise ALFWorld command from the admissible actions list \"\n    \"(e.g. go to desk 1, pick up pen 2, open drawer 1).\\n\"\n    \"Do NOT wrap the action in any tags. Do NOT output natural language descriptions \"\n    \"or anything after the action.\\n\\n\"\n    \"Example: &lt;think>[Assessment: neutral] I need to find a pen. The desk is a likely location. \"\n    \"[Suggestion: go to desk 1]&lt;/think>go to desk 1\\n\"\n)\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = \"You're a helpful assistant. \" + ENV_INSTRUCTION\nprint(\"\\n=== SYSTEM PROMPT (first 100 chars) ===\")\nprint(SYSTEM_PROMPT[:100])\nEOF",

The format looks correct and matches the AlfWorldMemory output exactly:

1. **System prompt**: `"You're a helpful assistant. " + env_instruction` ✓
2. **User message (turn 0)**: 
   - Task description ✓
   - Current observation (cleaned) ✓
   - Admissible actions ✓
   - Format instructions matching AlfWorldMemory ✓
3. **User message (turn 3)**:
   - Task description ✓
   - Step count ✓
   - Compact history with [Observation N, Action N] format ✓
   - Current observation ✓
   - Admissible actions ✓
   - Format instructions ✓

I'm noticing the task description appears twice in turn 0 — once from the task_desc field and again within the observation itself. Looking at how AlfWorldMemory's `_clean_observation` works, it strips out the welcome message and format-related text, but the task description embedded in the observation isn't being removed, which creates this duplication.

Actually, the task is only extracted and stored once from the initial state, so subsequent turns shouldn't have this problem since their observations won't contain the task description. In my eval script, I'm pulling the task from the first observation and reusing it for all calls, so the duplication should only happen on that first turn.

Let me verify that AlfWorldMemory handles this the same way—checking if the clean_observation function strips out the task line. It doesn't, so the task would appear both at the top and in the current observation for turn 0, which matches what I'm seeing. This duplication is expected and consistent with the training behavior.

Now I should check the rest of the script to make sure the diff doesn't have any other issues.
